<script>
    // HARDCODED DIRECTOR CREDENTIALS
    const ADMIN_USER = "Kaige";
    const ADMIN_PASS = "@Sammyzzz3Jimbo21";

    // AUTHENTICATION CHECK
    function checkAdminAuth() {
        const isAuthed = localStorage.getItem('pantheon_admin_auth') === 'true';
        const gate = document.getElementById('admin-auth-gate');
        const dash = document.getElementById('admin-dashboard');

        if (isAuthed) {
            gate.classList.add('hidden');
            dash.classList.remove('hidden');
            checkLockdownStatus();
            loadSiteConfig();
        } else {
            gate.classList.remove('hidden');
            dash.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }

    function authenticateAdmin(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const u = document.getElementById('auth-username').value.trim();
        const p = document.getElementById('auth-password').value.trim();
        const err = document.getElementById('auth-error');

        if (u.toLowerCase() === ADMIN_USER.toLowerCase() && p === ADMIN_PASS) {
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_admin_auth', 'true');
            err.style.display = 'none';
            checkAdminAuth();
        } else {
            err.style.display = 'block';
        }
    }

    function lockAdminSession() {
        localStorage.removeItem('pantheon_admin_auth');
        checkAdminAuth();
    }

    // TAB SWITCHING
    function switchTab(tabName) {
        document.getElementById('tab-security').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('tab-builder').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('tab-users').classList.add('hidden');

        document.getElementById('tab-security-btn').classList.remove('active');
        document.getElementById('tab-builder-btn').classList.remove('active');
        document.getElementById('tab-users-btn').classList.remove('active');

        if (tabName === 'security') {
            document.getElementById('tab-security').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('tab-security-btn').classList.add('active');
        } else if (tabName === 'builder') {
            document.getElementById('tab-builder').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('tab-builder-btn').classList.add('active');
        } else if (tabName === 'users') {
            document.getElementById('tab-users').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('tab-users-btn').classList.add('active');
            renderUserRoster();
        }
    }

    // LOCKDOWN CONTROLS
    function checkLockdownStatus() {
        const isLocked = localStorage.getItem('pantheon_lockdown') === 'true';
        const indicator = document.getElementById('lockdown-state-indicator');
        const btn = document.getElementById('master-kill-btn');

        if (isLocked) {
            indicator.innerText = 'LOCKDOWN_ENGAGED // OPERATIONS_FROZEN';
            indicator.style.color = 'var(--db-red)';
            btn.innerText = 'UNFREEZE TIME NEST OPERATIONS';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--db-ki)';
            btn.style.color = '#000000';
        } else {
            indicator.innerText = 'STUDIO_NORMAL';
            indicator.style.color = 'var(--db-ki)';
            btn.innerText = 'FREEZE TIME NEST OPERATIONS';
            btn.style.background = 'var(--db-red)';
            btn.style.color = '#ffffff';
        }
    }

    function toggleMasterLockdown() {
        const isLocked = localStorage.getItem('pantheon_lockdown') === 'true';
        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_lockdown', !isLocked ? 'true' : 'false');
        checkLockdownStatus();
        alert(!isLocked ? 'TIME NEST OPERATIONS FROZEN. SITE RESTRICTIONS ACTIVE.' : 'LOCKDOWN DISENGAGED. NORMAL OPERATIONS RESTORED.');
    }

    // PURGE PROTOCOLS
    function purgeComms() {
        if (confirm('PURGE ALL COMMUNITY CHAT TRANSMISSIONS FROM LOCAL MEMORY?')) {
            localStorage.removeItem('pantheon_comms');
            alert('COMMUNITY CHAT LOGS CLEARED.');
        }
    }

    function purgeDMs() {
        if (confirm('PURGE ALL HERO DIRECT MESSAGES FROM LOCAL MEMORY?')) {
            localStorage.removeItem('pantheon_dms');
            alert('DIRECT MESSAGES WIPED.');
        }
    }

    function purgeVault() {
        if (confirm('PURGE ALL MEDIA VAULT ASSETS FROM LOCAL MEMORY?')) {
            localStorage.removeItem('pantheon_media');
            alert('MEDIA VAULT PURGED.');
        }
    }

    function nuclearReset() {
        const verification = prompt('TYPE "OVERRIDE-PURGE" TO CONFIRM FULL SITE FACTORY RESET:');
        if (verification === 'OVERRIDE-PURGE') {
            localStorage.clear();
            alert('STUDIO MEMORY WIPED.');
            location.reload();
        } else {
            alert('VERIFICATION FAILED. OVERRIDE ABORTED.');
        }
    }

    // SITE BUILDER & CONFIG SAVING
    function loadSiteConfig() {
        const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_site_config') || '{}');
        if (cfg.primaryColor) document.getElementById('cfg-primary-color').value = cfg.primaryColor;
        if (cfg.accentColor) document.getElementById('cfg-accent-color').value = cfg.accentColor;
        if (cfg.bgColor) document.getElementById('cfg-bg-color').value = cfg.bgColor;
        if (cfg.siteTitle) document.getElementById('cfg-site-title').value = cfg.siteTitle;
        if (cfg.customCss) document.getElementById('cfg-custom-css').value = cfg.customCss;

        if (cfg.modules) {
            document.getElementById('mod-radar').checked = cfg.modules.radar !== false;
            document.getElementById('mod-lattice').checked = cfg.modules.lattice !== false;
            document.getElementById('mod-showcase').checked = cfg.modules.showcase !== false;
            document.getElementById('mod-logs').checked = cfg.modules.logs !== false;
        }
    }

    function saveSiteConfig(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_site_config') || '{}');
        
        cfg.primaryColor = document.getElementById('cfg-primary-color').value;
        cfg.accentColor = document.getElementById('cfg-accent-color').value;
        cfg.bgColor = document.getElementById('cfg-bg-color').value;
        cfg.siteTitle = document.getElementById('cfg-site-title').value.trim();

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_site_config', JSON.stringify(cfg));
        alert('VISUAL PARAMETERS SAVED TO STUDIO CONFIG.');
    }

    function saveModulesAndScripts() {
        const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_site_config') || '{}');
        
        cfg.customCss = document.getElementById('cfg-custom-css').value;
        cfg.modules = {
            radar: document.getElementById('mod-radar').checked,
            lattice: document.getElementById('mod-lattice').checked,
            showcase: document.getElementById('mod-showcase').checked,
            logs: document.getElementById('mod-logs').checked
        };

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_site_config', JSON.stringify(cfg));
        alert('LAYOUT MODULES & CUSTOM SCRIPTS INJECTED INTO STUDIO CONFIG.');
    }

    // HERO ROSTER MANAGEMENT
    function renderUserRoster() {
        const tbody = document.getElementById('user-roster-body');
        const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_operatives') || '{}');
        const userKeys = Object.keys(users);

        if (userKeys.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center; color:#8a9ba8;">NO REGISTERED HEROES FOUND IN MEMORY.</td></tr>';
            return;
        }

        tbody.innerHTML = userKeys.map(k => {
            const u = users[k];
            return `
                <tr>
                    <td style="font-weight:bold; color:#ffffff;">${u.callsign}</td>
                    <td>${u.role || 'Hero Fighter'}</td>
                    <td style="color:var(--db-ki);">${u.clearance || 'CLASS_01'}</td>
                    <td>${new Date(u.registered || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                    <td>
                        <button class="action-btn-danger" onclick="purgeOperative('${u.callsign}')">REMOVE HERO</button>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `;
        }).join('');
    }

    function purgeOperative(callsign) {
        if (confirm(`REMOVE HERO [${callsign}] FROM REGISTERED ROSTER?`)) {
            const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_operatives') || '{}');
            delete users[callsign];
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_operatives', JSON.stringify(users));
            renderUserRoster();
        }
    }

    // INITIALIZATION
    checkAdminAuth();
</script>

</body>
</html>

=== FILE: agent_hub.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pantheon Studios operational control hub.

Provides a menu for updating distribution strategy config and listing
intelligence logs in the current workspace.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import glob


CONFIG_FILE = "agent_config.md"


def prompt_non_empty(label: str) -> str:
    while True:
        value = input(label).strip()
        if value:
            return value
        print("Value cannot be empty. Please try again.")


def save_distribution_config(workspace: Path) -> Path:
    print("\nAdjust Distribution Strategy")
    print("-" * 30)

    strategy_name = prompt_non_empty("Strategy name: ")
    target_channels = prompt_non_empty("Target channels (comma-separated): ")
    cadence = prompt_non_empty("Distribution cadence (e.g., daily, weekly): ")
    notes = input("Optional notes: ").strip() or "N/A"

    content = f"""# Agent Distribution Configuration

- Updated At: {datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}
- Strategy: {strategy_name}
- Channels: {target_channels}
- Cadence: {cadence}
- Notes: {notes}
"""
    path = workspace / CONFIG_FILE
    path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def list_intelligence_logs(workspace: Path) -> list[Path]:
    pattern = str(workspace / "intelligence_log_*.md")
    return sorted(Path(p) for p in glob.glob(pattern))


def display_logs(workspace: Path) -> None:
    logs = list_intelligence_logs(workspace)
    print("\nIntelligence Logs")
    print("-" * 17)
    if not logs:
        print("No intelligence logs found.")
        return

    for idx, file_path in enumerate(logs, start=1):
        print(f"{idx}. {file_path.name}")


def main() -> None:
    workspace = Path.cwd()

    while True:
        print("\nPantheon Studios - Agent Hub")
        print("=" * 29)
        print("1. Adjust distribution strategy")
        print("2. List intelligence logs")
        print("3. Exit")

        choice = input("Select an option (1-3): ").strip()

        if choice == "1":
            config_path = save_distribution_config(workspace)
            print(f"Configuration saved: {config_path.name}")
        elif choice == "2":
            display_logs(workspace)
        elif choice == "3":
            print("Exiting Agent Hub.")
            break
        else:
            print("Invalid option. Please choose 1, 2, or 3.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

<script>
    // UPDATE SLIDER NUMBER DISPLAY
    function updateVal(id, value) {
        document.getElementById(id).innerText = value;
    }

    // LOAD ALL SAVED HEROES INTO SELECTOR DROPDOWN
    function populateSelector() {
        const selector = document.getElementById('char-selector');
        const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_characters') || '{}');
        
        // Keep "NEW" as first option
        selector.innerHTML = '<option value="NEW">[ CREATE NEW HERO ]</option>';

        // Default Kaige if not in saved
        if (!saved['kaige']) {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = 'kaige';
            opt.innerText = 'KAIGE (Default Lead)';
            selector.appendChild(opt);
        }

        Object.keys(saved).forEach(id => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = id;
            opt.innerText = `${saved[id].callsign.toUpperCase()} (${saved[id].showTitle || 'Show Fighter'})`;
            selector.appendChild(opt);
        });
    }

    // LOAD SELECTED HERO INTO FORM FOR EDITING
    function loadSelectedHero() {
        const selectedId = document.getElementById('char-selector').value;
        const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_characters') || '{}');

        if (selectedId === 'NEW') {
            document.getElementById('hero-builder-form').reset();
            return;
        }

        const hero = saved[selectedId] || {
            id: "kaige",
            callsign: "KAIGE",
            realName: "RESTRICTED",
            showTitle: "Project: Phantom Frequency",
            role: "Lead Heavy Strike / Spatial Titan",
            race: "Frieza Race",
            status: "UNLEASHED / ACTIVE",
            height: "Twice Frieza's Height",
            avatarUrl: "",
            bio: "Kaige is a Frieza Race character standing nearly twice the height of a standard model. He runs a dark aura with a green core and black undertones, and rarely uses Golden Form—saving it for specific high-stakes moments rather than relying on it every match.",
            narrative: "He serves as the primary background antagonist and main character driving events behind the scenes in Dragonball Legacy Reborn (DBLR). Rather than being front-and-center constantly, he operates from the shadows, stepping in directly only when it matters to test other characters.",
            capabilities: "Built around spatial control with a primary focus on Ki blasts. Uses the Center of Gravity (COG) system to track opponent movement, heavy Ki blasts to break defense meters, and timed counters like Dark Inscription or Shadow Crusher to intercept aggressive rushes.",
            communityLore: "A long-time veteran in the 160+ member DBLR community operating under blackbasilisk42. The character persona pre-dates official Black Frieza media. He keeps a low profile in public chats, so when he shows up in lobbies or group calls, people take notice.",
            skills: [
                { label: "MAX HEALTH", value: 95 },
                { label: "MAX KI", value: 85 },
                { label: "MAX STAMINA", value: 90 },
                { label: "BASIC ATTACK", value: 88 },
                { label: "STRIKE SUPER", value: 98 },
                { label: "KI BLAST SUPER", value: 75 }
            ],
            equipment: ["Dark Inscription", "Center of Gravity System", "Gigantic Nova", "Strike Boost Super Soul"]
        };

        // Populate Text Fields
        document.getElementById('field-callsign').value = hero.callsign || '';
        document.getElementById('field-realName').value = hero.realName || '';
        document.getElementById('field-showTitle').value = hero.showTitle || '';
        document.getElementById('field-race').value = hero.race || '';
        document.getElementById('field-height').value = hero.height || '';
        document.getElementById('field-role').value = hero.role || '';
        document.getElementById('field-status').value = hero.status || 'UNLEASHED / ACTIVE';
        document.getElementById('field-avatarUrl').value = hero.avatarUrl || '';
        document.getElementById('field-bio').value = hero.bio || '';
        document.getElementById('field-narrative').value = hero.narrative || hero.showRole || '';
        document.getElementById('field-capabilities').value = hero.capabilities || '';
        document.getElementById('field-communityLore').value = hero.communityLore || '';

        // Populate Attributes Sliders
        const skillMap = {};
        if (hero.skills) {
            hero.skills.forEach(s => skillMap[s.label] = s.value);
        }

        document.getElementById('stat-health').value = skillMap['MAX HEALTH'] || 85;
        document.getElementById('val-health').innerText = skillMap['MAX HEALTH'] || 85;

        document.getElementById('stat-ki').value = skillMap['MAX KI'] || 80;
        document.getElementById('val-ki').innerText = skillMap['MAX KI'] || 80;

        document.getElementById('stat-stamina').value = skillMap['MAX STAMINA'] || 90;
        document.getElementById('val-stamina').innerText = skillMap['MAX STAMINA'] || 90;

        document.getElementById('stat-basic').value = skillMap['BASIC ATTACK'] || 88;
        document.getElementById('val-basic').innerText = skillMap['BASIC ATTACK'] || 88;

        document.getElementById('stat-strike').value = skillMap['STRIKE SUPER'] || 95;
        document.getElementById('val-strike').innerText = skillMap['STRIKE SUPER'] || 95;

        document.getElementById('stat-kiblast').value = skillMap['KI BLAST SUPER'] || 75;
        document.getElementById('val-kiblast').innerText = skillMap['KI BLAST SUPER'] || 75;

        // Populate Equipment Tag Cloud
        document.getElementById('field-equipment').value = hero.equipment ? hero.equipment.join(', ') : '';
    }

    // SAVE / UPDATE CHARACTER IN LOCALSTORAGE
    function saveCharacter(e) {
        e.preventDefault();

        const callsign = document.getElementById('field-callsign').value.trim();
        const heroId = callsign.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '_');

        const equipmentRaw = document.getElementById('field-equipment').value;
        const equipmentArray = equipmentRaw.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s.length > 0);

        const heroObj = {
            id: heroId,
            callsign: callsign,
            realName: document.getElementById('field-realName').value.trim() || 'Restricted',
            showTitle: document.getElementById('field-showTitle').value.trim(),
            race: document.getElementById('field-race').value.trim() || 'Unspecified Race',
            height: document.getElementById('field-height').value.trim() || 'N/A',
            role: document.getElementById('field-role').value.trim() || 'Hero Fighter',
            status: document.getElementById('field-status').value,
            avatarUrl: document.getElementById('field-avatarUrl').value.trim(),
            bio: document.getElementById('field-bio').value.trim(),
            narrative: document.getElementById('field-narrative').value.trim(),
            capabilities: document.getElementById('field-capabilities').value.trim(),
            communityLore: document.getElementById('field-communityLore').value.trim(),
            skills: [
                { label: "MAX HEALTH", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-health').value) },
                { label: "MAX KI", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-ki').value) },
                { label: "MAX STAMINA", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-stamina').value) },
                { label: "BASIC ATTACK", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-basic').value) },
                { label: "STRIKE SUPER", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-strike').value) },
                { label: "KI BLAST SUPER", value: parseInt(document.getElementById('stat-kiblast').value) }
            ],
            equipment: equipmentArray.length > 0 ? equipmentArray : ["Standard Issue Ki Modules"]
        };

        const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_characters') || '{}');
        saved[heroId] = heroObj;
        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_characters', JSON.stringify(saved));

        // Show Success Banner
        const banner = document.getElementById('status-banner');
        banner.style.display = 'block';
        banner.innerHTML = `
            HERO DOSSIER [${callsign}] PUBLISHED SUCCESSFULLY! 
            <a href="dossier.html?id=${heroId}" target="_blank">[OPEN HERO WIKI PAGE ↗]</a>
        `;

        populateSelector();
        document.getElementById('char-selector').value = heroId;
    }

    // INITIALIZATION
    populateSelector();
    const requestedHeroId = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('id');
    if (requestedHeroId && document.querySelector(`#char-selector option[value="${requestedHeroId}"]`)) {
        document.getElementById('char-selector').value = requestedHeroId;
        loadSelectedHero();
    }
</script>

=== FILE: crawlers.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pantheon Studios autonomous intelligence crawler.

Fetches a URL using a custom User-Agent, analyzes text density, and writes
structured intelligence logs in Markdown format.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import re

import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from modules.crawler_engine import CrawlerEngine


TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 20


@dataclass
class CrawlReport:
    url: str
    title: str
    fetched_at: str
    status_code: int
    word_count: int
    character_count: int
    paragraph_count: int
    avg_words_per_paragraph: float
    content_density: float
    top_keywords: list[tuple[str, int]]
    snippet: str


def slugify(value: str, max_length: int = 80) -> str:
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s-]", "", value).strip().lower()
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[\s_-]+", "-", cleaned)
    slug = cleaned.strip("-") or "untitled"
    return slug[:max_length].rstrip("-")


def normalize_whitespace(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()


def fetch_page(url: str) -> tuple[requests.Response, dict[str, str], float]:
    engine = CrawlerEngine(timeout_seconds=TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
    crawl_response = engine.fetch(url)
    return crawl_response.response, crawl_response.used_headers, crawl_response.delay_seconds


def analyze_html(url: str, response: requests.Response) -> CrawlReport:
    soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, "html.parser")

    for tag in soup(["script", "style", "noscript", "svg", "meta", "link"]):
        tag.extract()

    title = normalize_whitespace(soup.title.string) if soup.title and soup.title.string else "Untitled Page"
    paragraphs = [normalize_whitespace(p.get_text(" ")) for p in soup.find_all("p")]
    paragraphs = [p for p in paragraphs if p]

    text = normalize_whitespace(soup.get_text(" "))
    words = re.findall(r"\b[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z-]{1,}\b", text.lower())
    word_count = len(words)

    stop_words = {
        "the", "and", "for", "with", "that", "this", "from", "have", "are", "was",
        "you", "your", "they", "their", "will", "would", "about", "there", "which",
        "when", "what", "where", "while", "into", "been", "more", "than", "also",
    }
    filtered_words = [w for w in words if w not in stop_words and len(w) > 3]
    top_keywords = Counter(filtered_words).most_common(10)

    char_count = len(text)
    paragraph_count = len(paragraphs)
    avg_words_per_paragraph = (word_count / paragraph_count) if paragraph_count else 0.0
    density = (char_count / len(response.text)) if response.text else 0.0
    snippet = text[:900] + ("..." if len(text) > 900 else "")

    return CrawlReport(
        url=url,
        title=title,
        fetched_at=datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        status_code=response.status_code,
        word_count=word_count,
        character_count=char_count,
        paragraph_count=paragraph_count,
        avg_words_per_paragraph=avg_words_per_paragraph,
        content_density=density,
        top_keywords=top_keywords,
        snippet=snippet,
    )


def render_report(report: CrawlReport, used_headers: dict[str, str], delay_seconds: float) -> str:
    keyword_lines = "\n".join(
        f"- {word}: {count}" for word, count in report.top_keywords
    ) or "- No strong keywords detected"

    return f"""# Intelligence Log: {report.title}

## Source
- URL: {report.url}
- Fetched At: {report.fetched_at}
- HTTP Status: {report.status_code}
- User-Agent: {used_headers.get('User-Agent', 'unknown')}
- Request Delay (seconds): {delay_seconds:.2f}

## Metrics
- Word Count: {report.word_count}
- Character Count: {report.character_count}
- Paragraph Count: {report.paragraph_count}
- Avg Words / Paragraph: {report.avg_words_per_paragraph:.2f}
- Content Density (visible chars / raw HTML chars): {report.content_density:.4f}

## Top Keywords
{keyword_lines}

## Content Snippet
{report.snippet}
"""


def save_report(
    report: CrawlReport,
    workspace: Path,
    used_headers: dict[str, str],
    delay_seconds: float,
) -> Path:
    filename = f"intelligence_log_{slugify(report.title)}.md"
    path = workspace / filename

    suffix = 1
    while path.exists():
        path = workspace / f"intelligence_log_{slugify(report.title)}_{suffix}.md"
        suffix += 1

    path.write_text(render_report(report, used_headers, delay_seconds), encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Pantheon Studios intelligence crawler")
    parser.add_argument("url", nargs="?", help="Target URL to crawl")
    return parser.parse_args()


def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()
    target_url = args.url or input("Enter target URL: ").strip()

    if not target_url:
        print("No URL provided. Exiting.")
        return

    try:
        response, used_headers, delay_seconds = fetch_page(target_url)
        report = analyze_html(target_url, response)
    except requests.RequestException as exc:
        print(f"Fetch failed: {exc}")
        return
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"Unexpected crawler error: {exc}")
        return

    output_path = save_report(report, Path.cwd(), used_headers, delay_seconds)
    print(f"Intelligence report created: {output_path.name}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: creator.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pantheon Studios content creator CLI.

Prompts for a title and post body, then saves a clean Markdown file locally.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import re


def slugify(value: str, max_length: int = 80) -> str:
    """Convert text to a filesystem-safe slug."""
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9\s-]", "", value).strip().lower()
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[\s_-]+", "-", cleaned)
    slug = cleaned.strip("-") or "untitled-post"
    return slug[:max_length].rstrip("-")


def prompt_multiline(prompt: str, sentinel: str = "END") -> str:
    """Capture multi-line input until the sentinel is entered on its own line."""
    print(prompt)
    print(f"Type '{sentinel}' on a new line when you are done.\n")
    lines: list[str] = []
    while True:
        try:
            line = input()
        except EOFError:
            break
        if line.strip() == sentinel:
            break
        lines.append(line)
    return "\n".join(lines).strip()


def build_markdown(title: str, content: str) -> str:
    """Create a clean Markdown representation of the post."""
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    return (
        f"# {title}\n\n"
        f"_Created: {timestamp}_\n\n"
        f"## Content\n\n"
        f"{content}\n"
    )


def save_post(title: str, content: str, workspace: Path) -> Path:
    """Save the generated markdown post and return its path."""
    slug = slugify(title)
    path = workspace / f"{slug}.md"

    # Avoid accidental overwrite by adding a numeric suffix if needed.
    suffix = 1
    while path.exists():
        path = workspace / f"{slug}_{suffix}.md"
        suffix += 1

    path.write_text(build_markdown(title, content), encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def main() -> None:
    workspace = Path.cwd()
    print("Pantheon Studios - Post Creator")
    print("=" * 33)

    while True:
        title = input("Enter post title: ").strip()
        if title:
            break
        print("Title cannot be empty. Please try again.")

    content = prompt_multiline("Enter your post content:")
    if not content:
        print("No content provided. Exiting without creating a file.")
        return

    path = save_post(title=title, content=content, workspace=workspace)
    print(f"\nPost saved successfully: {path.name}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

<script>
    const STORAGE_KEY = 'pantheon_dossier_kaige';

    // Format plain text into styled HTML paragraphs and bullet points
    function formatText(text) {
        if (!text) return '';
        let lines = text.split('\n');
        let html = '';
        let inList = false;

        lines.forEach(line => {
            let trimmed = line.trim();
            if (trimmed.startsWith('•') || trimmed.startsWith('-')) {
                if (!inList) { html += '<ul>'; inList = true; }
                html += `<li>${trimmed.substring(1).trim()}</li>`;
            } else {
                if (inList) { html += '</ul>'; inList = false; }
                if (trimmed.length > 0) {
                    html += `<p>${trimmed}</p>`;
                }
            }
        });
        if (inList) html += '</ul>';
        return html;
    }

    function saveAndPublish() {
        const data = {
            title: document.getElementById('in-title').value,
            classification: document.getElementById('in-class').value,
            race: document.getElementById('in-race').value,
            height: document.getElementById('in-height').value,
            tag: document.getElementById('in-tag').value,
            affil: document.getElementById('in-affil').value,
            photo: document.getElementById('in-photo').value,
            sec1: document.getElementById('in-sec1').value,
            sec2: document.getElementById('in-sec2').value,
            sec3: document.getElementById('in-sec3').value,
            sec4: document.getElementById('in-sec4').value,
            sec5: document.getElementById('in-sec5').value
        };

        // Save to Browser Storage
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
        
        render(data);
        switchToView();
    }

    function render(data) {
        document.getElementById('out-title').innerText = data.title;
        document.getElementById('out-class').innerText = data.classification;
        document.getElementById('out-race').innerText = data.race;
        document.getElementById('out-height').innerText = data.height;
        document.getElementById('out-tag').innerText = data.tag;
        document.getElementById('out-affil').innerText = data.affil;
        
        const photoElem = document.getElementById('out-photo');
        if (data.photo) {
            photoElem.src = data.photo;
        } else {
            photoElem.style.display = 'none';
        }

        document.getElementById('out-sec1').innerHTML = formatText(data.sec1);
        document.getElementById('out-sec2').innerHTML = formatText(data.sec2);
        document.getElementById('out-sec3').innerHTML = formatText(data.sec3);
        document.getElementById('out-sec4').innerHTML = formatText(data.sec4);
        document.getElementById('out-sec5').innerHTML = formatText(data.sec5);
    }

    function loadSavedData() {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (saved) {
            const data = JSON.parse(saved);
            document.getElementById('in-title').value = data.title || '';
            document.getElementById('in-class').value = data.classification || '';
            document.getElementById('in-race').value = data.race || '';
            document.getElementById('in-height').value = data.height || '';
            document.getElementById('in-tag').value = data.tag || '';
            document.getElementById('in-affil').value = data.affil || '';
            document.getElementById('in-photo').value = data.photo || '';
            document.getElementById('in-sec1').value = data.sec1 || '';
            document.getElementById('in-sec2').value = data.sec2 || '';
            document.getElementById('in-sec3').value = data.sec3 || '';
            document.getElementById('in-sec4').value = data.sec4 || '';
            document.getElementById('in-sec5').value = data.sec5 || '';
            
            render(data);
            switchToView();
        }
    }

    function switchToView() {
        document.getElementById('editor').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('view-dossier').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('btn-save').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('btn-edit').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function switchToEdit() {
        document.getElementById('editor').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('view-dossier').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('btn-save').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('btn-edit').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    // Auto load on open
    window.onload = loadSavedData;
</script>

</body>
</html>

    function initPage() {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const heroId = urlParams.get('id');

        // Merge default database with any custom creations saved in localStorage
        const savedCharacters = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_characters') || '{}');
        const allHeroes = { ...defaultHeroes, ...savedCharacters };

        const container = document.getElementById('dossier-content');

        // IF NO HERO SELECTED: Render Character Selection Grid
        if (!heroId || !allHeroes[heroId]) {
            renderRosterGrid(container, allHeroes);
        } else {
            // IF HERO SELECTED: Render That Hero's Dossier
            renderDossierView(container, allHeroes[heroId]);
        }
    }

    // 1. ROSTER SELECTION MENU VIEW
    function renderRosterGrid(container, heroes) {
        const heroKeys = Object.keys(heroes);

        const cardsHTML = heroKeys.map(key => {
            const h = heroes[key];
            const initials = h.callsign.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
            const avatar = h.avatarUrl ? `<img src="${h.avatarUrl}" alt="${h.callsign}">` : initials;

            return `
                <div class="roster-card">
                    <div class="roster-header">
                        <div class="roster-avatar">${avatar}</div>
                        <div class="roster-meta">
                            <div class="roster-name">${h.callsign}</div>
                            <div class="roster-role">${h.role || 'Unspecified Role'}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="roster-spec">
                        <div><strong>RACE:</strong> ${h.race || 'N/A'}</div>
                        <div><strong>PROJECT:</strong> ${h.showTitle || 'N/A'}</div>
                        <div><strong>GAMERTAG:</strong> ${h.primaryGamertag || 'N/A'}</div>
                    </div>
                    <a href="dossier.html?id=${h.id}" class="roster-select-btn">[ VIEW DOSSIER ]</a>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');

        container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-header">
                    <span>SELECT CHARACTER // TIME NEST ARCHIVES</span>
                    <a href="builder.html" style="color:var(--db-gold); font-size:0.75rem; text-decoration:none;">[ CREATE NEW CHARACTER ↗ ]</a>
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:0.85rem; color:#8a9ba8; margin-bottom:10px;">
                    Select an active character profile below to view full scouter attributes, deep lore, and narrative saga arcs.
                </div>
                <div class="roster-grid">
                    ${cardsHTML}
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }

    // 2. SINGLE CHARACTER DOSSIER VIEW
    function renderDossierView(container, hero) {
        const initials = hero.callsign.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
        const avatarHTML = hero.avatarUrl 
            ? `<img src="${hero.avatarUrl}" alt="${hero.callsign}">` 
            : initials;

        container.innerHTML = `
            <!-- HERO HEADER CARD -->
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-header">
                    <a href="dossier.html" style="color:var(--db-cyan); font-size:0.8rem; text-decoration:none; font-weight:bold;">[ ← BACK TO ROSTER SELECT ]</a>
                    <a href="builder.html?id=${hero.id}" style="color:var(--db-gold); font-size:0.75rem; text-decoration:none;">[EDIT IN HERO BUILDER ↗]</a>
                </div>

                <div class="hero-banner">
                    <div class="hero-avatar">${avatarHTML}</div>
                    <div class="hero-meta">
                        <h1 class="hero-callsign">${hero.callsign}</h1>
                        <div class="hero-subtitle">${hero.role}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="spec-grid">
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">SHOW PROJECT</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.showTitle || 'N/A'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">GAMERTAG</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.primaryGamertag || 'N/A'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">RACE / SPECIES</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.race || 'Unspecified'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">HEIGHT</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.height || 'N/A'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">AFFILIATION</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.affiliation || 'Pantheon Core'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="spec-item">
                        <span class="spec-label">REAL NAME</span>
                        <span class="spec-val">${hero.realName || 'Restricted'}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- DEEP LORE CARD -->
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-header">DEEP LORE & SHOW ARCS</div>
                
                <div class="lore-block">
                    <div class="lore-title">1. OVERVIEW & COMMUNITY MYTHOS</div>
                    <div class="lore-text">${hero.bio || 'No backstory registered.'}</div>
                </div>

                <div class="lore-block">
                    <div class="lore-title">2. SHOW SAGA & NARRATIVE TRAJECTORY</div>
                    <div class="lore-text">${hero.narrative || hero.showRole || 'No show arc registered.'}</div>
                </div>

                <div class="lore-block">
                    <div class="lore-title">3. COMBAT CAPABILITIES & SPATIAL SYSTEMS</div>
                    <div class="lore-text">${hero.capabilities || 'No combat systems registered.'}</div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- XENOVERSE ATTRIBUTES & EQUIPMENT CARD -->
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-header">XENOVERSE ATTRIBUTES & SIGNATURE SKILLS</div>
                
                <div class="grid-2">
                    <!-- POWER GRID METERS -->
                    <div class="stat-bar-group">
                        <div style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--db-gold); font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:6px;">POWER GRID METERS</div>
                        ${(hero.skills || []).map(s => `
                            <div class="stat-row">
                                <span class="stat-label">${s.label}</span>
                                <div class="stat-track">
                                    <div class="stat-fill" style="width: ${s.value}%;"></div>
                                </div>
                                <span class="stat-num">${s.value}</span>
                            </div>
                        `).join('')}
                    </div>

                    <!-- SIGNATURE EQUIPMENT TAGS -->
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--db-gold); font-weight:bold; margin-bottom:12px;">SIGNATURE SKILLS & GEAR</div>
                        <div class="tag-cloud">
                            ${(hero.equipment || []).map(item => `
                                <span class="eq-tag">[ ${item} ]</span>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `;
    }

    initPage();
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    const activeCallsign = localStorage.getItem('pantheon_active_session') || 'GUEST_FAN';

    // MAINTENANCE GATE CHECK LOGIC
    const DEV_PASSCODE = '@jimbo21'; // Passkey to bypass maintenance overlay

    function checkMaintenanceGate() {
        const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const passParam = urlParams.get('pass');
        const overlay = document.getElementById('maintenance-overlay');

        if (passParam === DEV_PASSCODE || localStorage.getItem('pantheon_maint_unlocked') === 'true') {
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_maint_unlocked', 'true');
            if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
            if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'flex';
        }
    }

    function unlockMaintenance(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const inputVal = document.getElementById('maint-key-input').value.trim();
        const errBox = document.getElementById('maint-error');

        if (inputVal === DEV_PASSCODE) {
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_maint_unlocked', 'true');
            document.getElementById('maintenance-overlay').style.display = 'none';
        } else {
            errBox.innerText = 'ACCESS DENIED: INVALID DEVELOPER PASSKEY';
        }
    }

    // Live Clock
    function updateClock() {
        const now = new Date();
        document.getElementById('clock').innerText = now.toISOString().substring(11, 19) + ' UTC';
    }
    setInterval(updateClock, 1000);
    updateClock();

    // Theme Switcher Toggle
    function toggleTheme() {
        const body = document.body;
        const btn = document.getElementById('theme-btn');
        if (body.classList.contains('theme-spectral')) {
            body.classList.remove('theme-spectral');
            body.classList.add('theme-obsidian');
            btn.innerText = '[MODE: SUPER SAIYAN]';
        } else {
            body.classList.remove('theme-obsidian');
            body.classList.add('theme-spectral');
            btn.innerText = '[MODE: KAI-ENERGY]';
        }
    }

    // Audio Toggle Mock
    let audioState = false;
    function toggleAudio() {
        audioState = !audioState;
        document.querySelector('.audio-btn').innerText = audioState ? '[AUDIO: ON]' : '[AUDIO: OFF]';
    }

    // Filter Hero Roster Cards
    function filterUnits(type, btn) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-group .tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        btn.classList.add('active');

        const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.dossier-card');
        cards.forEach(card => {
            if (type === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-type') === type) {
                card.style.display = 'block';
            } else {
                card.style.display = 'none';
            }
        });
    }

    // Terminal Input
    function execTerminal(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const input = document.getElementById('term-input');
        const cmd = input.value.trim();
        if (!cmd) return;

        const box = document.getElementById('terminal-box');
        const line = document.createElement('div');
        line.className = 'sys-line';
        line.innerHTML = `CHAT: [${activeCallsign}]: ${cmd}`;
        box.appendChild(line);
        box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
        input.value = '';
    }

    // Load Public Uploaded Feed
    function loadPublicFeed() {
        const feed = document.getElementById('public-feed');
        const allMedia = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_media') || '[]');
        const allComments = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_comments') || '{}');

        const publicMedia = allMedia.filter(m => m.privacy === 'public');

        if (publicMedia.length === 0) {
            feed.innerHTML = `
                <div class="feed-card" style="text-align:center; color:#8a9ba8; font-size:0.8rem;">
                    No active media uploads broadcasted yet. Upload assets marked <strong>PUBLIC_HQ</strong> from your profile to populate this feed.
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        feed.innerHTML = publicMedia.map(item => {
            const comments = allComments[item.id] || [];
            
            return `
                <div class="feed-card">
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px;">
                        <span style="color:#fff; font-weight:bold; font-size:0.95rem;">${item.title}</span>
                        <span style="color:#8a9ba8; font-size:0.75rem;">DISPATCHED BY: [${item.owner}] // ${new Date(item.timestamp).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>

                    <img class="media-preview" src="${item.data}" alt="${item.title}">

                    <div class="comments-block">
                        <div style="font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--db-ki); margin-bottom:8px;">COMMUNITY FEEDBACK // COMMENTS (${comments.length})</div>
                        
                        <div style="display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px; max-height:140px; overflow-y:auto; margin-bottom:10px;">
                            ${comments.length === 0 
                                ? '<div style="color:#607080; font-size:0.75rem;">No comments logged yet.</div>' 
                                : comments.map(c => `
                                    <div style="font-size:0.75rem; border-bottom:1px dotted rgba(0,229,255,0.15); padding-bottom:4px;">
                                        <span style="color:var(--db-gold); font-weight:bold;">[${c.author}]</span>
                                        <span style="color:#607080; font-size:0.65rem; margin-left:6px;">${new Date(c.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                                        <div style="color:#d0e0f0; margin-top:2px;">${c.text}</div>
                                    </div>
                                `).join('')
                            }
                        </div>

                        <form style="display:flex; gap:10px;" onsubmit="postComment(event, '${item.id}')">
                            <input type="text" id="input-${item.id}" placeholder="Leave feedback on this episode..." required autocomplete="off" style="flex:1; background:rgba(6,9,19,0.9); border:1px solid rgba(0,229,255,0.4); color:#fff; padding:8px; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:0.8rem; outline:none;">
                            <button type="submit" style="background:var(--db-gold); color:#000; border:none; padding:8px 16px; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer; text-transform:uppercase; font-size:0.75rem;">TRANSMIT</button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');
    }

    function postComment(e, mediaId) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const input = document.getElementById(`input-${mediaId}`);
        const text = input.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        const allComments = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_comments') || '{}');
        if (!allComments[mediaId]) {
            allComments[mediaId] = [];
        }

        allComments[mediaId].push({
            author: activeCallsign,
            text: text,
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
        });

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_comments', JSON.stringify(allComments));
        input.value = '';
        loadPublicFeed();
    }

    // APPLY DYNAMIC VISUAL & MODULE CONFIG FROM ADMIN STUDIO
    function applySiteConfig() {
        const cfg = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_site_config') || '{}');
        
        if (cfg.primaryColor) document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--db-cyan', cfg.primaryColor);
        if (cfg.accentColor) document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--db-gold', cfg.accentColor);
        if (cfg.bgColor) document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--bg-void', cfg.bgColor);
        
        if (cfg.siteTitle) {
            const banner = document.getElementById('site-title-banner');
            if (banner) banner.innerHTML = `<div class="pulse-orb"></div> ${cfg.siteTitle}`;
        }

        if (cfg.modules) {
            const radar = document.getElementById('mod-radar-card');
            const lattice = document.getElementById('mod-lattice-card');
            const showcase = document.getElementById('mod-showcase-card');
            const logs = document.getElementById('mod-logs-card');

            if (radar) radar.style.display = cfg.modules.radar === false ? 'none' : 'block';
            if (lattice) lattice.style.display = cfg.modules.lattice === false ? 'none' : 'block';
            if (showcase) showcase.style.display = cfg.modules.showcase === false ? 'none' : 'block';
            if (logs) logs.style.display = cfg.modules.logs === false ? 'none' : 'block';
        }

        if (cfg.customCss) {
            let styleTag = document.getElementById('pantheon-custom-css');
            if (!styleTag) {
                styleTag = document.createElement('style');
                styleTag.id = 'pantheon-custom-css';
                document.head.appendChild(styleTag);
            }
            styleTag.innerHTML = cfg.customCss;
        }
    }

    // INITIALIZATION
    checkMaintenanceGate();
    applySiteConfig();
    loadPublicFeed();
</script>

</body>
</html>

        <button class="tab-btn active" id="btn-login" onclick="switchTab('login')">[SIGN IN]</button>

<script>
    function switchTab(tab) {
        const loginBtn = document.getElementById('btn-login');
        const regBtn = document.getElementById('btn-register');
        const loginForm = document.getElementById('form-login');
        const regForm = document.getElementById('form-register');
        const status = document.getElementById('status-message');

        status.innerText = '';

        if (tab === 'login') {
            loginBtn.classList.add('active');
            regBtn.classList.remove('active');
            loginForm.classList.remove('hidden');
            regForm.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            regBtn.classList.add('active');
            loginBtn.classList.remove('active');
            regForm.classList.remove('hidden');
            loginForm.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }

    function getUsers() {
        return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_operatives') || '{}');
    }

    function handleRegister(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const callsign = document.getElementById('reg-callsign').value.trim();
        const passkey = document.getElementById('reg-passkey').value;
        const role = document.getElementById('reg-role').value;
        const clearance = document.getElementById('reg-clearance').value;
        const status = document.getElementById('status-message');

        const users = getUsers();

        if (users[callsign]) {
            status.className = 'status-msg error';
            status.innerText = 'CALLSIGN ALREADY REGISTERED IN TIME NEST.';
            return;
        }

        users[callsign] = {
            callsign: callsign,
            passkey: passkey,
            role: role,
            clearance: clearance,
            registered: new Date().toISOString(),
            status: 'ACTIVE_TIME_PATROLLER'
        };

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_operatives', JSON.stringify(users));
        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_active_session', callsign);

        status.className = 'status-msg success';
        status.innerText = 'TIME PATROLLER AWAKENED. SYNCHRONIZING PROFILE...';

        setTimeout(() => {
            window.location.href = 'profile.html';
        }, 1200);
    }

    function handleLogin(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const callsign = document.getElementById('login-callsign').value.trim();
        const passkey = document.getElementById('login-passkey').value;
        const status = document.getElementById('status-message');

        const users = getUsers();
        const user = users[callsign];

        if (!user || user.passkey !== passkey) {
            status.className = 'status-msg error';
            status.innerText = 'ACCESS DENIED: INVALID CALLSIGN OR KI PASSKEY.';
            return;
        }

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_active_session', callsign);

        status.className = 'status-msg success';
        status.innerText = 'KI SYNCHRONIZED. ACCESSING HERO DOSSIER...';

        setTimeout(() => {
            window.location.href = 'profile.html';
        }, 1000);
    }
</script>

</body>
</html>

<script>
    let activeCallsign = localStorage.getItem('pantheon_active_session');
    let currentMode = 'broadcast';

    if (!activeCallsign) {
        window.location.href = 'login.html';
    }

    function loadProfile() {
        const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_operatives') || '{}');
        const user = users[activeCallsign];

        if (!user) {
            logout();
            return;
        }

        document.getElementById('disp-callsign').innerText = user.callsign;
        document.getElementById('disp-role').innerText = user.role || 'Hero Fighter';
        document.getElementById('disp-clearance').innerText = user.clearance || 'CLASS_01';
        document.getElementById('disp-status').innerText = user.status || 'ACTIVE_TIME_PATROLLER';
        document.getElementById('disp-date').innerText = new Date(user.registered || Date.now()).toLocaleDateString();

        // Load Socials Data
        const socials = user.socials || {};
        document.getElementById('input-discord').value = socials.discord || '';
        document.getElementById('input-youtube').value = socials.youtube || '';
        document.getElementById('input-patreon').value = socials.patreon || '';
        document.getElementById('input-facebook').value = socials.facebook || '';

        renderSocialBadges(socials);

        populateRecipientLists(users);
        loadBroadcast();
        loadMediaGallery();
    }

    function renderSocialBadges(socials) {
        const container = document.getElementById('disp-socials-container');
        container.innerHTML = '';
        let count = 0;

        if (socials.discord) {
            container.innerHTML += `<span class="social-badge">[DISCORD] ${socials.discord}</span>`;
            count++;
        }
        if (socials.youtube) {
            const url = socials.youtube.startsWith('http') ? socials.youtube : `https://${socials.youtube}`;
            container.innerHTML += `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" class="social-badge">[YOUTUBE] ↗</a>`;
            count++;
        }
        if (socials.patreon) {
            const url = socials.patreon.startsWith('http') ? socials.patreon : `https://${socials.patreon}`;
            container.innerHTML += `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" class="social-badge">[PATREON] ↗</a>`;
            count++;
        }
        if (socials.facebook) {
            const url = socials.facebook.startsWith('http') ? socials.facebook : `https://${socials.facebook}`;
            container.innerHTML += `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" class="social-badge">[FACEBOOK] ↗</a>`;
            count++;
        }

        if (count === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#607080; font-size:0.75rem;">NO ENERGY RELAYS CONFIGURED</span>';
        }
    }

    function toggleSocialEdit() {
        const form = document.getElementById('social-edit-form');
        form.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function saveSocials(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const users = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_operatives') || '{}');
        if (!users[activeCallsign]) return;

        users[activeCallsign].socials = {
            discord: document.getElementById('input-discord').value.trim(),
            youtube: document.getElementById('input-youtube').value.trim(),
            patreon: document.getElementById('input-patreon').value.trim(),
            facebook: document.getElementById('input-facebook').value.trim()
        };

        localStorage.setItem('pantheon_operatives', JSON.stringify(users));
        renderSocialBadges(users[activeCallsign].socials);
        toggleSocialEdit();
    }

    function populateRecipientLists(users) {
        const dmSelect = document.getElementById('dm-target');
        const mediaTargetSelect = document.getElementById('media-target-user');
        
        dmSelect.innerHTML = '';
        mediaTargetSelect.innerHTML = '';

        const otherOperatives = Object.keys(users).filter(c => c !== activeCallsign);

        if (otherOperatives.length === 0) {
            dmSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">NO OTHER HEROES</option>';
            mediaTargetSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">NO OTHER HEROES</option>';
            return;
        }

        otherOperatives.forEach(c => {
            const opt1 = document.createElement('option');
            opt1.value = c;
            opt1.innerText = c;
            dmSelect.appendChild(opt1);

            const opt2 = document.createElement('option');
            opt2.value = c;
            opt2.innerText = c;
            mediaTargetSelect.appendChild(opt2);
        });
    }

    function toggleRestrictedTarget() {
        const privacy = document.getElementById('media-privacy').value;
        const wrapper = document.getElementById('restricted-wrapper');
        if (privacy === 'restricted') {
            wrapper.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            wrapper.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }

    function setMode(mode) {
        currentMode = mode;
        const bBtn = document.getElementById('tab-broadcast');
        const dBtn = document.getElementById('tab-dm');
        const dmBar = document.getElementById('dm-recipient-bar');

        if (mode === 'broadcast') {
            bBtn.classList.add('active');
            dBtn.classList.remove('active');
            dmBar.classList.add('hidden');
            loadBroadcast();
        } else {
            dBtn.classList.add('active');
            bBtn.classList.remove('active');
            dmBar.classList.remove('hidden');
            loadDMs();
        }
    }

    function loadBroadcast() {
        const msgs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_comms') || '[]');
        const box = document.getElementById('comms-box');
        
        if (msgs.length === 0) {
            box.innerHTML = '<div style="color: #607080;">No broadcast transmissions logged.</div>';
            return;
        }

        box.innerHTML = msgs.map(m => `
            <div class="msg-entry">
                <span class="msg-sender">[${m.sender}]</span>
                <span class="msg-time">${new Date(m.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                <div class="msg-body">${m.text}</div>
            </div>
        `).join('');

        box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    }

    function loadDMs() {
        const target = document.getElementById('dm-target').value;
        const box = document.getElementById('comms-box');

        if (!target) {
            box.innerHTML = '<div style="color: #607080;">Register a second hero to test Telepathic Direct Messaging.</div>';
            return;
        }

        const allDms = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_dms') || '[]');
        const thread = allDms.filter(m => 
            (m.sender === activeCallsign && m.recipient === target) ||
            (m.sender === target && m.recipient === activeCallsign)
        );

        if (thread.length === 0) {
            box.innerHTML = `<div style="color: #607080;">Encrypted channel open with [${target}]. No signals exchanged yet.</div>`;
            return;
        }

        box.innerHTML = thread.map(m => `
            <div class="msg-entry">
                <span class="msg-sender ${m.sender === activeCallsign ? 'dm-me' : ''}">[${m.sender} -> ${m.recipient}]</span>
                <span class="msg-time">${new Date(m.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                <div class="msg-body">${m.text}</div>
            </div>
        `).join('');

        box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
    }

    function postMessage(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const input = document.getElementById('msg-input');
        const text = input.value.trim();
        if (!text) return;

        if (currentMode === 'broadcast') {
            const msgs = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_comms') || '[]');
            msgs.push({
                sender: activeCallsign,
                text: text,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
            });
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_comms', JSON.stringify(msgs));
            loadBroadcast();
        } else {
            const target = document.getElementById('dm-target').value;
            if (!target) return;

            const allDms = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_dms') || '[]');
            allDms.push({
                sender: activeCallsign,
                recipient: target,
                text: text,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
            });
            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_dms', JSON.stringify(allDms));
            loadDMs();
        }

        input.value = '';
    }

    function uploadMedia(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const title = document.getElementById('media-title').value.trim();
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('media-file');
        const privacy = document.getElementById('media-privacy').value;
        const targetUser = document.getElementById('media-target-user').value;

        if (!fileInput.files || !fileInput.files[0]) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = function(evt) {
            const mediaData = evt.target.result;
            const allMedia = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_media') || '[]');

            allMedia.push({
                id: 'media_' + Date.now(),
                owner: activeCallsign,
                title: title,
                data: mediaData,
                privacy: privacy, // 'private', 'restricted', 'public'
                targetUser: privacy === 'restricted' ? targetUser : null,
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
            });

            localStorage.setItem('pantheon_media', JSON.stringify(allMedia));
            document.getElementById('media-title').value = '';
            fileInput.value = '';
            loadMediaGallery();
        };

        reader.readAsDataURL(fileInput.files[0]);
    }

    function loadMediaGallery() {
        const gallery = document.getElementById('media-gallery');
        const allMedia = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('pantheon_media') || '[]');

        const visibleMedia = allMedia.filter(item => {
            if (item.owner === activeCallsign) return true;
            if (item.privacy === 'public') return true;
            if (item.privacy === 'restricted' && item.targetUser === activeCallsign) return true;
            return false;
        });

        if (visibleMedia.length === 0) {
            gallery.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#607080; grid-column:span 2;">No show assets found in capsule vault. Upload an image above to test.</div>';
            return;
        }

        gallery.innerHTML = visibleMedia.map(item => {
            let badgeClass = 'badge-private';
            let badgeText = 'CLASSIFIED CAPSULE';

            if (item.privacy === 'public') {
                badgeClass = 'badge-public';
                badgeText = 'PUBLIC SHOWCASE';
            } else if (item.privacy === 'restricted') {
                badgeClass = 'badge-restricted';
                badgeText = `RESTRICTED -> ${item.targetUser}`;
            }

            return `
                <div class="media-item">
                    <img src="${item.data}" alt="${item.title}">
                    <div class="media-title">${item.title}</div>
                    <span class="media-badge ${badgeClass}">${badgeText}</span>
                    <div style="font-size:0.65rem; color:#607080; margin-top:4px;">BY: [${item.owner}]</div>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');
    }

    function logout() {
        localStorage.removeItem('pantheon_active_session');
        window.location.href = 'login.html';
    }

    loadProfile();
</script>

</body>
</html>

=== FILE: PROJECT_STATE.md ===
# PANTHEON STUDIOS - WORKSPACE CONTEXT & STATE

## 1. Project Architecture & Current Status
- **Main Project:** `Pantheon_Studios` (Backend tools, `Modelfile`, `modules/preflight.py`, `.venv`).
- **Web Strategy:** All old broken web server launchers (`run.py`, `site_launcher.py`, `.bat` wrappers) were removed.
- **Standalone Public Site:** Located in `standalone_team_site/`. Completely isolated HTML/CSS/JS with no backend dependencies.
- **Future Admin Portal:** Will be built later as a separate middleman layer between the standalone public site and the backend.

## 2. Public Site Visual & Design System
- **Theme:** Cyberpunk Tactical HUD / Sci-Fi Portal.
- **Palette:**
  - Background Void: `#05070a`
  - Neon Emerald (Status/Telemetry): `#00ff9d`
  - Neon Cyan (Borders/Buttons/Reticles): `#00e5ff`
  - Neon Pink (Media/Highlights): `#ff007f`
- **Features:** CRT scanlines, angled clip-paths, live UTC clock, tactical filter buttons, terminal comms simulator, audio synthesizer, and interactive HUD cursor.

## 3. Strict Development Rules for AI Assistant
- **Safety Boundary:** Never execute OS-level shutdown, reboot, or system-wide commands. Keep all edits inside `Pantheon_Studios`.
- **No Over-Engineering:** Avoid multi-file background launcher wrappers, complex PID managers, or daemon loops.
- **Standalone Preservation:** Keep `standalone_team_site/` self-contained. Do not re-attach web runners to root backend code unless explicitly requested.

=== FILE: synthesizer.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Pantheon Studios intelligence synthesizer.

Aggregates all intelligence logs and produces a consolidated workspace update.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import re


OUTPUT_FILE = "pantheon_synthesized_update.md"


def extract_title(content: str, fallback: str) -> str:
    for line in content.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("# "):
            return line.removeprefix("# ").strip()
    return fallback


def extract_snippet(content: str, fallback_length: int = 500) -> str:
    match = re.search(r"## Content Snippet\s*(.+)", content, flags=re.DOTALL)
    if match:
        raw = match.group(1).strip()
        return raw[:fallback_length] + ("..." if len(raw) > fallback_length else "")

    cleaned = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", content).strip()
    return cleaned[:fallback_length] + ("..." if len(cleaned) > fallback_length else "")


def collect_logs(workspace: Path) -> list[Path]:
    return sorted(workspace.glob("intelligence_log_*.md"))


def compile_report(workspace: Path, logs: list[Path]) -> Path:
    lines: list[str] = []
    lines.append("# Pantheon Synthesized Intelligence Update")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append(f"- Generated At: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
    lines.append(f"- Logs Processed: {len(logs)}")
    lines.append("")

    if not logs:
        lines.append("No intelligence logs were found in the workspace.")
    else:
        for idx, log_file in enumerate(logs, start=1):
            content = log_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
            title = extract_title(content, fallback=log_file.stem)
            snippet = extract_snippet(content)

            lines.append(f"## {idx}. {title}")
            lines.append(f"- Source File: {log_file.name}")
            lines.append("")
            lines.append("### Snippet")
            lines.append(snippet)
            lines.append("")

    output_path = workspace / OUTPUT_FILE
    output_path.write_text("\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return output_path


def main() -> None:
    workspace = Path.cwd()
    logs = collect_logs(workspace)
    output_path = compile_report(workspace, logs)
    print(f"Synthesis complete: {output_path.name}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: .github\copilot-instructions.md ===
# Pantheon Studios Copilot Instructions

All future code modifications must be applied directly within workspace files.
Do not provide standalone replacement code blocks in chat when the request is to implement or edit code.
Use direct file edits so the repository remains the source of truth.

Human-in-the-Loop policy is mandatory:
- New generated content should enter queue/pending/ first unless explicitly marked otherwise by a human decision.
- Only content approved by a human should remain in queue/approved/ or be published onward.
- Rejected content should be moved to queue/rejected/ with rejection context retained when possible.

Platform extension rule:
- All future publishing or distribution platform extensions must be implemented under publishers/.
- Shared publishing abstractions should extend publishers/base_publisher.py.

# pytest cache directory #

This directory contains data from the pytest's cache plugin,
which provides the `--lf` and `--ff` options, as well as the `cache` fixture.

**Do not** commit this to version control.

See [the docs](https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/cache.html) for more information.

# Privacy and Copyright Policy

[Fair enough—let's skip relying on Copilot's agent context for this one and get the exact text straight into your hands.

Here is the complete, full text of the revised policy, ready for you to copy and paste directly into your `lore/privacy_and_copyright.md` file:

---

# Pantheon Studios — Master Governance, IP & Operational Privacy Policy

*(Public-Facing Statement)*

## Introduction

Pantheon Studios is an independent creative studio focused exclusively on the creation, curation, and dissemination of digital narratives, lore, and public media. This document sets forth the binding policies governing all public-facing assets, publications, and external interactions associated with Pantheon Studios.

By accessing, reading, referencing, or sharing any content produced or published by Pantheon Studios, all external parties agree to adhere strictly to the terms, security protocols, and intellectual property conditions outlined herein.

---

## 1. Operational Confidentiality & Non-Disclosure

> **ABSOLUTE NOTICE:** Pantheon Studios operates under strict internal confidentiality. Under no circumstances will Pantheon Studios disclose, confirm, or discuss its internal workflows, underlying technology, infrastructure, operational methods, or the personal identities of its creators.

* **Strict Non-Disclosure:** Any and all inquiries, requests, or investigative attempts regarding internal mechanisms, automation pipelines, tools, software stacks, or back-end operations are strictly denied and will remain unacknowledged.
* **Prohibition of Backend Scrutiny:** **Any attempt to probe, trace, scan, reverse-engineer, decompile, de-anonymize, or investigate the underlying origins, technological framework, or personnel behind Pantheon Studios is explicitly prohibited.**
* **Identity & Personal Privacy:** Pantheon Studios maintains absolute separation between its published entities and internal individuals. **No personal identifying information (PII) regarding studio personnel, operators, or contributors will ever be published, confirmed, or disclosed.**

---

## 2. Privacy & Data Non-Collection

Pantheon Studios does not operate user account systems, run user-facing databases, or harvest personal data.

* **Zero User Data Collection:** Pantheon Studios does not collect, store, buy, sell, or process personal identification data, financial records, or private contact details from external audiences.
* **Passive Third-Party Engagement:** Any interaction with Pantheon Studios content occurring on third-party platforms (e.g., Substack, Reddit, Medium, X) is governed strictly by the privacy policies of those respective platforms. Pantheon Studios does not extract or aggregate private user profiles from these channels.
* **No Tracking Infrastructure:** Pantheon Studios does not deploy cookies, tracking pixels, or fingerprinting scripts designed to identify individual readers.

---

## 3. Intellectual Property, Copyright & Fair Use Governance

### 3.1 Studio Intellectual Property

All original creative assets produced by Pantheon Studios—including unique narrative lore, original scripts, custom analytical frameworks, studio branding, logos, press briefs, and published text—are the **100% exclusive intellectual property of Pantheon Studios**.

### 3.2 Third-Party Property Disclaimer & Fair Use

Pantheon Studios frequently analyzes, references, and incorporates third-party media elements (including game assets, graphics, video footage, audio clips, trademarks, and established franchise lore) within its creative publications.

* **Non-Claim of Third-Party Assets:** Pantheon Studios **does not claim ownership, copyright, or trademark rights** over underlying third-party intellectual property, visual assets, game graphics, character models, or registered trademarks referenced or displayed within studio materials. All such assets remain the exclusive property of their respective copyright and trademark owners.
* **Ownership of Transformative Work & Usage:** Pantheon Studios **does claim full ownership and copyright over its original usage, creative framing, editorial commentary, unique lore integration, compilation, and narrative analysis** built around or utilizing those third-party elements.
* **Fair Use Standard:** All inclusion of third-party media, graphics, or intellectual property is conducted strictly under applicable **Fair Use doctrine** (e.g., transformative editorial commentary, critique, news reporting, educational breakdown, or narrative customization). No endorsement or affiliation with third-party copyright holders is implied.

### 3.3 Limited Personal License

External readers are granted a limited, revocable, non-exclusive license to view, quote, and share published content solely for personal, non-commercial, and informational purposes, provided that proper attribution to Pantheon Studios is maintained for original studio materials.

### 3.4 Prohibited Commercial Exploitation

**No third party may reproduce, re-publish, sell, license, modify, claim ownership of, or commercially exploit any original narrative, character, text, or compiled asset created by Pantheon Studios without explicit written authorization.**

### 3.5 Misrepresentation & Impersonation

Third parties are strictly prohibited from posing as official representatives, partners, or internal personnel of Pantheon Studios.

---

## 4. Security, Access & Anti-Interference Policy

Pantheon Studios enforces a zero-tolerance policy regarding security breaches, network probing, or operational interference.

* **Prohibited Network Activity:** Any automated or manual attempt to exploit, disrupt, denial-of-service (DoS), scrape at malicious volumes, or gain unauthorized access to studio servers, repositories, or private distribution channels is illegal and strictly prohibited.
* **Countermeasures & Enforcement:** Violations of system security or attempts to compromise studio stealth will result in immediate IP bans, blacklisting from distribution feeds, reporting to platform security teams, and potential legal action where applicable.

---

## 5. Third-Party Media & Syndication Guidelines

Pantheon Studios frequently releases sanitized media kits, trend briefs, and press summaries designed for independent writers, journalists, and media outlets.

* **Syndication Usage:** Independent creators, journalists, and media publishers are permitted to reference, analyze, and expand upon publicly released Pantheon Studios briefs and lore, provided the core text is not intentionally altered to misrepresent the studio's stance.
* **No Implied Endorsement:** Coverage or reference to Pantheon Studios content by independent writers does not constitute an official endorsement, partnership, or joint venture between Pantheon Studios and said third parties.

---

## 6. Policy Governance & Updates

Pantheon Studios reserves the right to modify, amend, or update this policy document at any time without prior notice. The current version published within official distribution channels represents the binding governance standard.
